Dropping the HNR draw shifts the later draws, so these are new clips, not train_v2ko's without aspiration; labels come from the same render. Not consolidated to disk as a set: audio and the D-081 oracle labels go to `cache/d082/`.
- *Features*: `Teacher.enc` of v3.1 (both seeds, D-079's checkpoints and method, whole clips padded to whole seconds).
- *Heads*: D-081 B's form and training (MLP, L1 on standardised targets, 4000 steps, head seeds 1 and 2), sonorant frames with finite labels.
- **C1, the confound**: the glottal slope (D-081 A1's measure) of a closed-F1 head on h, against the label ceiling recomputed on the no-aspiration sets (A2's full inputs, boosted trees). Reported: the closed label's own slope on these frames, and h + <a>, h + <a> + df1. **Artefact explanation confirmed** if h's slope is at most the ceiling + 0.05 on both trunks; **refuted** (h carries more than labels can) if at least the ceiling + 0.15 on both; otherwise unclear.
- **C2, learnability**: heads on h for (a) the shift s = log(F1 acoustic / F1 closed) and (b) df1. Measure: R² beyond the vowel part. The vowel part g is a boosted-tree fit of the target from the true acoustic F1-F4 on the training frames; the score is R²(target - g, prediction - g) on val. Held-out R² has 0 as its null. **Learnable** if at least 0.3 on both trunks (each the head-seed mean); **not learnable** if under 0.1 on both; otherwise partly. Reported: the label-only reference (the same measure for a boosted fit from A2's full perfect labels), the seed spread, and the shift head's glottal slope with p = F1 acoustic (true) x exp(-s hat).
- *What follows*: C2 learnable -> the screening run's registration carries a shift head (or paired acoustic / closed heads) as the closed-F1 question. Not learnable on frozen features -> the screening run may still carry it, as a trained-trunk question, with this result as its prior. C1 decides whether D-081's ceiling use (the fourth use of the oracle step) stands.
- Cost: render about 20 min (CPU, 24 workers), features about 15 min (GPU), heads about 20 min (GPU).
- Scripts: `scripts/analysis/d082_probe.py` (render, features, heads, decide); reports `reports/analysis/d082/`.
- *Result (2026-11-06; `reports/analysis/d082/decide_d082.txt`, `heads_v31_s{1,2}.json`)*: **C1 unclear; C2 shift not learnable; df1 not learnable** (on frozen v3.1 features).
  - C1 (glottal slope, trunks 1 / 2): the closed label reads 0.887, the label ceiling 0.288. h reads 0.370 / 0.356, down from D-081's 0.47 with aspiration. That is +0.08 / +0.07 above the ceiling: between the rule's bounds. Most of h's excess over the ceiling in D-081 went with the aspiration; a small part remains, too small to call. h + <a> 0.43 / 0.40; h + <a> + df1 1.12 / 1.08 (near-arithmetic, as D-081's note says).
  - C2: R² beyond the vowel part is -0.23 / -0.21 for the shift and -0.35 for df1 (seed spread under 0.04), against a label-only reference of +0.37 and +0.26. The vowel part alone explains R² 0.43 (shift) and 0.49 (df1). Reported: the shift head's glottal slope is 0.18 / 0.19, below h's.
  - *Reading (results seen)*: negative R² means the heads' predictions minus the true-label vowel part vary more than the residual itself. Part of this is the measure: the heads read the vowel part from audio, with error, and that error counts against them. So "not learnable" here is "no recoverable signal beyond the vowel part on frozen features, by this measure", not proof of absence. The shift head's slope (0.18, under h's 0.36) points the same way. A trained trunk is the open question (the registered "what follows").

## D-083 (registered 2026-11-06; seen beforehand: D-081 B5 / A3, D-082, no D-083 result): does the aVTL head gain from a *predicted* iVTL track, and from its context?

*Status*: registered (Hannah, 2026-11-06). In the teacher the aVTL and iVTL heads are independent siblings: per-frame MLPs on the same trunk feature, with no link and no context of their own (`teacher.py`; v3.1 has the aVTL head only). D-081 found that the *true* iVTL cuts the aVTL head's MAE by 20 % (B5) and that 2 s of iVTL context cuts a label-only fit's by 53 % (A3). This is the "is it learnable?" step: the same with the iVTL a head predicts from v3.1's features. Errors in that prediction carry over, so the gain can only shrink.
- *Data*: D-081's (train_v2ko's first 3000 clips, val_v2ko; D-079's cached v3.1 features, both seeds; D-081's labels). The aspiration artefact concerns the closed F1, not the length labels; it is noted, not removed.
- *Predicted quantities* (D-081 B's head form and training; head seeds 1 and 2): an iVTL head on h (log `length_inst_cm`), and an aVTL head on h (log `length_cm`). Train-set predictions are out of fold (two folds by clip parity), so later fits never see in-sample predictions; val predictions average the two folds' heads. Reported: the iVTL head's MAE.
- **P1, the linked head**: an aVTL head on h; on h + true iVTL (B5 again); on h + predicted iVTL (detached, as an input). **Usable** if h + predicted cuts the MAE by at least 5 % against h, beyond 3x the head-seed spread, on both trunks. Reported: the share of the true-iVTL gain kept.
- **P2, context**: boosted trees (A3's) for `length_cm` from window statistics (A3's: min, mean, max, 10 / 90 % over centred windows of 0, 0.25, 0.5, 1 and 2 s) of (i) the aVTL head's own prediction (the control: clip context without the iVTL); (ii) (i) plus the predicted iVTL; (iii) (i) plus the true iVTL (reference). **iVTL context helps** if (ii)'s 2 s MAE is at most 0.9x (i)'s 2 s MAE on both trunks. Reported: the curves.
- *What follows*: P1 usable -> the screening run links the heads (aVTL reads a detached iVTL). P2 helps -> aVTL gets a context-aware input over the iVTL track (pooling or attention), which motivates long crops. If (i) alone captures most of A3's context gain, plain clip-level averaging of aVTL is the cheaper design.
- Cost: heads about 20 min (GPU); trees about 30 min (CPU).
- Scripts: `scripts/analysis/d083_vtl_link.py`; reports `reports/analysis/d083/`.
- *Result (2026-11-06; `reports/analysis/d083/decide_d083.txt`, `v31_s{1,2}.json`)*: **P1 not usable; P2 iVTL context no clear help.**
  - The frozen iVTL head's MAE is 0.19 cm. P1 (aVTL MAE, trunks 1 / 2): h 0.157 / 0.155; + true iVTL 0.125 / 0.124; **+ predicted iVTL 0.157 / 0.155 (+0.1 / +0.5 %; none of the true gain kept)**.
  - P2 at 2 s: (i) aVTL's own context 0.142 / 0.142; (ii) + predicted iVTL 0.141 / 0.141 (0.99x); (iii) + true iVTL 0.078 / 0.077. aVTL's own context gives about -9 % (0.155 -> 0.142), most of it by 0.25 s and flat after 1 s.
  - *Reading*: a head on the same frozen features adds nothing the aVTL head can't already read from them. The true iVTL's gain is information that is not in v3.1's h. So linking the heads on this trunk is pointless; it helps only if a trained trunk learns the iVTL well (the true-input ceiling, 0.125, is the landmark). Clip-level averaging of aVTL is the cheap, real gain (-9 %), and it saturates by 1 s on 2-4 s clips. A trained iVTL head (screening run) is still worth carrying, since D-081's ceilings show the iVTL is informative; its own accuracy is what limits the link.

## D-084 (registered 2026-11-06; seen beforehand: D-073, D-081-D-083, no D-084 result): what do the other formants tell a formant head, and does v3.1's chain pass F1's errors on?

*Status*: registered (Hannah, 2026-11-06). In v3.1 (flat read-out) the formant heads are a chain. Head Fn reads h plus the predicted means of F1..Fn-1 (not detached), and a soft mask (strength 8) pushes it above the predicted previous formant. That is the comb's structure (a later head consuming an earlier estimate), but trained on its own predictions only, with no truth phase. D-073's joint read-out (learned queries with self-attention among the four, frozen trunk) matched the chain and gained nothing. This entry asks the oracle question D-073 did not: how much do the true other formants say about Fn beyond h, and does the chain carry F1's errors into F2-F4?
- *Data*: D-081's (D-079's cached v3.1 features for both trunks; D-081's labels; train_v2ko's first 3000 clips, val_v2ko, sonorant frames). Target: the closed formants (v3.1's label).
- **M, the oracle matrix** (frozen features; D-081 B's head form and training, head seeds 1 and 2): per target Fn (n = 1-4), a head on h; on h + each true Fm (m != n) singly; on h + all three. MAE in Hz. *Label-only reference*: boosted trees for Fn from the true other formants without h (D-081 A2's form), so "what the others imply across vowels and lengths" is split from "what h already knows". **Informative** cell (n, m): h + Fm cuts Fn's MAE by at least 5 %, beyond 3x the head-seed spread, on both trunks.
- **S, the swap check** (v3.1's own chain, both trunks, whole val_v2ko clips as D-079's features): v3.1's heads re-run with the means fed to later heads (and to the mask) replaced by the true formants: (S1) true F1 only; (S2) true F1..Fn-1 for each Fn. Reported: F2-F4 MAE against the unchanged chain. Sensitivity: F1's fed mean scaled by 0.97 and 1.03; the median |d Fn| / |d F1| for n = 2-4. **The chain passes F1's errors on** if S1 cuts F2's MAE by at least 5 % on both trunks.
- *What follows*: no informative cells and S1 null -> the chain, mask and joint heads are not where the error lives (consistent with D-073); a truth ramp on the formant chain is not worth an arm. Informative cells or S1 positive -> a truth ramp on the chain's fed means (and a detached variant) becomes a candidate arm, registered separately. M's ceilings are a frozen bound: a predicted sibling from the same frozen h adds nothing (D-083), so M shows what a trained joint head could at most use, not what it would learn.
- Cost: M about 40 min (GPU, 20 arms x 2 seeds x 2 trunks), trees about 10 min (CPU); S about 10 min (GPU).
- Scripts: `scripts/analysis/d084_formant_links.py`; reports `reports/analysis/d084/`.
- *Note (2026-11-06, before any result)*: the first chain run failed at once on a padding call in the swap check (`d084_formant_links.py`); fixed (numpy edge padding), nothing scored before.
- *Result (2026-11-06; `reports/analysis/d084/decide_d084.txt`, `M_*.json`, `S_*.json`)*: **informative cells F1 <- F4, F2 <- F1, F3 <- F2, F4 <- F1; S1 no: v3.1's chain does not use its fed means at all.**
  - M (MAE Hz, trunks 1 / 2): all three true others cut each formant's MAE by 10-16 % (F1 31.6 -> 27.1 / 31.0 -> 27.2; F2 -11 / -10 %; F3 -14 / -13 %; F4 -16 / -14 %). The strongest single cells: F4 <- F1 (-11 / -10 %), F3 <- F2 (-10 / -9 %), F1 <- F4 (-8 / -7 %), F2 <- F1 (-5 / -5 %). F1 <- F2 just misses on trunk 2 (-4.3 %). The label-only reference (the true others without h) is far worse (F1 85 Hz, F2 358 Hz), so the gain is the others combined with h, not the vowel space alone.
  - S: feeding the true F1 changes F2-F4 by at most 0.1 Hz; with the true F1..Fn-1, F3 and F4 move by up to 0.7 Hz (through the mask). Scaling F1's fed mean by 0.97 or 1.03 moves F2-F4 by a median of 0.000 of F1's shift. The chain re-run reproduces the model's own read-out (checked on the first clip).
  - *Reading (results seen)*: the heads receive the earlier means but have learned to ignore them. The input is the bin index / 368, so a 3 % F1 change moves it by about 0.007, next to h's 128 standardised channels. Only the mask acts, and only when a formant would fall below the one before it. So the chain is not passing F1's errors on, and a truth ramp on the chain as built would do nothing: the heads don't read the input it would correct. The true other formants are worth 10-16 % per formant at the frozen ceiling. D-083's lesson says a sibling's prediction from the same frozen h adds nothing, so that ceiling is reachable only if training makes the formants' estimates better together (shared gradients, a joint loss, or a rescaled, ramped input), which is a trained-arm question. Consistent with D-073: a joint read-out on the frozen trunk gained nothing.

## D-085 (DRAFT, 2026-11-06; not registered, nothing runs from it): a screening run that relaxes several constraints at once

*Status*: draft for Hannah's review. Her proposal (2026-11-05): a run "not worried about maintaining lineage", which gives the model what might help (a wider trunk, more heads, longer context) and asks several questions at once. The design was discussed on 2026-11-05 (loss weighting, what might hurt, a neutral arm; an advisor review), and D-081-D-084's oracle and learnability checks set which heads it carries. Open items (end of entry) must be closed before registration.

*Questions, each with its primary measure fixed in advance; everything else reported at the same detail*:
- **Q1, is the closed-phase F1 learnable?** The glottal slope of F1 (D-078's addendum) on aspiration-free coupled validation; the uncoupled offset between the closed and acoustic heads; KL's gap growth (D-074). Landmarks: label ceiling 0.29 (D-082), frozen v3.1 0.36, full recovery 0.89 on that scale.
- **Q2, the aVTL-iVTL split.** aVTL MAE on synthetic validation, D-051's measures, probe 10 (whether the anatomical head follows the instantaneous length). Landmarks (D-083): frozen v3.1 0.155 cm; true iVTL as input 0.125; true iVTL with 2 s context 0.078.
- **Q3, real-voice F1.** PTDB-TUG women's closed-phase bias and Hillenbrand F1 (dev), as always; guards on F2-F4, f0 RPA and resynthesised validation.
- **Q4, capacity and context.** The base arm against v3.1 on all of the above: does the wider trunk with long context help at all?

*Base (shared by every arm)*:
- *Trunk*: about 1.5x v3.1's width (conformer_dim 128 -> 192, embed 384 -> 576; the rest as v3.1), the learning rate scaled by the width rule, and at least v3.1's step budget (D-040's recipe), so a negative isn't "undertrained".
- *Context*: mixed crops: most batches as now (up to 4 s); some at 4-16 s, batched by a frame budget. Long material comes from new synthetic clips of 8-16 s with one anatomy per clip (the cleanest teacher for the split). Resynthesised clips are at most 6 s now (VocalSet and Zeroth train sets: median 3.7 / 4.0 s, max 6.0); they stay as they are. Long crops come from synthetic clips only (Hannah, 2026-11-06): only they carry exact aVTL and iVTL labels, and real-voice transfer is measured on held-out real recordings anyway. A longer resynthesis is worth doing only if the base arm shows long context helps. Attention uses rotary positions, so 1600 frames needs no new positional scheme.
- *Render*: train_v2's mix plus D-080's coupled share. A new opt-in flag sends the cascade's aspiration through the coupled tract (D-081's artefact); the golden renders stay identical.
- *Evaluation at both lengths*: 4 s crops and long, so a model that needs long context to work is visible.

*Arms (single seed each; a factorial on the shared base)*:

| Arm | Heads bundle | Neutral bundle |
|---|---|---|
| Base | - | - |
| Heads | yes | - |
| (Neutral and Both: dropped, open item 1) | | |

If the neutral list has fewer than 3 qualifying items, it is dropped and the run is two arms: base and heads. The interaction cell is reported, not decided (one seed).

*Heads bundle (each with D-081-D-084's evidence)*:
- *Paired formant sets*: closed and acoustic F1-F4 as two main tasks (never cut against each other), the shift reported as their difference. D-081: the offset is audible and the shift, not the opening, is the missing quantity; D-082: not readable from frozen v3.1, so this is the trained-trunk question.
- *A trained iVTL head* beside aVTL, with aVTL reading a detached iVTL whose source ramps from true to predicted, as the f0 comb does. D-081 B5 / A3: the true iVTL is worth 20 % (53 % with context); D-083: a frozen sibling's prediction adds nothing, so the link pays only if training learns iVTL well.
- *Not in this run: the formant chain with standardised fed means and a truth ramp* (Hannah, 2026-11-06). It changes the main formant read-out and would move every measure here, so it was tested on its own in 5b's decay branch (D-086, not a candidate on either arm, 2026-11-07, so it stays out of the base; D-084: the true other formants are worth 10-16 % per formant at the frozen ceiling, and v3.1's chain ignores its fed means). If it helps, it joins the base of the next run.
- *Not carried*: the opening head (D-080: an Rd proxy, costs 3-9 % formant accuracy); aperiodicity heads (D-081 B3: -0.3 %); source heads for bandwidths (B4: -2 %). Clip-level averaging of aVTL (D-083: -9 %) is post-processing, reported for every arm.
- *Not carried (Hannah, 2026-11-06)*: frame level and spectral slope. No oracle evidence, no renderer in this run, and known risks (level against the gain augmentation; slope overlaps source shape, D-080 / D-081 B4). They belong with the differentiable renderer's registration.

*Neutral bundle*: changes with a "no difference" or "within noise" verdict whose interval still includes a useful effect, and no failed guard. The rule is applied to the decision log before the list is read, and the list is fixed in the registration.

*Loss weighting*: each auxiliary loss normalised by its running mean once past its detached phase; a fixed auxiliary budget (about 25 % of the total) split per concept (length, coupling / shift), not per head; the main tasks' weights unchanged. Every head uses the side-path pattern (detached first, then ramped in). No gradient surgery (advisor: untested code, and fixed weights match adaptive methods in the literature).

*Reading*:
- A positive is attributed to the bundle in its arm; a negative is weak (one harmful component could mask a useful one). To make negatives readable, every head's learning curve and the main tasks' validation are logged every 1000 steps.
- Thresholds from known seed spreads (one seed per arm, so a signal must clear about twice the seed-to-seed difference to be broken down later). 5b's two seeds (`d040_5b_s{1,2}_b20k`) differ by 1.1 / 0.9 / 1.7 / 2.5 Hz on synthetic F1-F4 MAE (3-5 %), 0.2 Hz on women's closed-phase F1 bias, 0.003-0.004 in probe 11's F1 slope and 0.034 in Hillenbrand length reliability; D-065's paired arms moved women's bias by up to 1.9 Hz between seeds. Proposed: the glottal slope >= +0.03 (twice its 0.015); synthetic MAE >= 8 % per formant or the mean >= 5 %; women's closed-phase |error| >= 4 Hz (twice D-065's spread; D-056's 2 Hz is within one seed); aVTL MAE >= 10 % (D-083's -9 % from clip averaging is reported apart); length reliability >= 0.07. Below these, a change is reported, not called.
- D-080's costs of coupled data (a -3 Hz offset on uncoupled audio, a worse F4) belong to the base, not the heads.

*Smoke test before the GPU budget is committed*: memory and step time at 1600 frames on the iGPU (chunked if needed), every head's loss moving, the main tasks holding over the first few hundred steps.

*Cost*: about 12-16 h per run at the wider size; the two arms run side by side, so about 16 h.

*Open items before registration*:
1. (Done, 2026-11-06.) The neutral list, by the rule, from a keyword search of the log's results ("no difference", "within noise", "not a candidate", "no gain", "neither", "not adopted", "not robust") and a reading of each hit: **D-056** (anchored read-out: non-inferior, synthetic -0.5 to -0.8 SE, no gain), **D-073 queries_abs** (query read-out: non-inferior, women's closed-phase bias -1.0 / -1.5 Hz on both seeds, under the 2 Hz gain), **D-065 bw** (bandwidths decoupled from the formants: guards held, primary half met). Excluded: D-072 (a tight null, both arms at 5b to the decimal, no useful effect left in the interval), D-065 gain and D-051 5c (failed guards), v3a / v3b (worse), D-031's weight decay and D-040's rB / rC (recipe values the base already fixes). D-056 and D-073 are both formant read-outs and can't be combined, and either would move every formant measure, as D-086 did for the chain. So at most two compatible items qualify, under the 3 needed: **the neutral arm is dropped; the run is two arms, base and heads.** The keyword search could miss an entry phrased otherwise; a full read would settle it.
2. (Closed, 2026-11-06: long crops from synthetic clips only.)
3. (Done, 2026-11-06, `fb7a06a`.) `SynthConfig.coupled_aspiration` / `voice.synthesize(coupled_aspiration=...)`; off by default, golden renders unchanged; tested (no pole movement: equals the closed tract; with movement: only the aspiration changes; no effect without coupling).
4. (Done, 2026-11-06: see the thresholds above, proposed.)
5. (Closed, 2026-11-06: the formant chain goes to D-086; level and slope heads out.)
6. The smoke test.

## D-086 (registered 2026-11-06; seen beforehand: D-084, a 2-step branch test of the code, D-086's decision script in check mode on D-065's arm, no arm result): the formant chain with standardised fed means, and a truth ramp, in 5b's decay branch

*Status*: registered (Hannah, 2026-11-06: taken out of D-085's heads bundle and tested alone). D-084: v3.1's formant heads receive the earlier formants' predicted means but ignore them (fed as bin / 368, a 3 % F1 change moves the input by about 0.007; sensitivity 0.000), while the true other formants are worth 10-16 % per formant at the frozen ceiling. Question: **with inputs that have leverage, does the chain use the other formants, and does forcing it from the labels at first help it learn to?**
- *Arm A, standardised* (`TeacherConfig.fed_standardised`): each later head Fn also reads the fed means of F1..Fn-1 as (bin - centre_m) / 30 (centres at about 500 / 1500 / 2500 / 3500 Hz; 30 bins = half an octave) through a fresh linear path (`fed_heads.*`), initialised at zero, so the branch starts exactly at 5b (tested). The raw path and the mask are unchanged. The new path trains in its own AdamW at `fed_lr` 1e-3 x the model's schedule (weight decay 0.01), as D-072's side path.
- *Arm B, A plus a truth ramp* (`TrainConfig.fed_truth_steps` 2000): the means fed to later heads and to the mask come from the formant labels (where defined; elsewhere the chain's own) with a probability decaying 1 -> 0 over the branch's first 2000 steps, so the last 2000 steps are on its own means, as the f0 comb's ramp.
- *Training*: `va train teacher --branch-from runs/d040_5b_s{s}/ckpt_016000.pt --decay-steps 4000 --train-set fed_standardised=true --train-set fed_lr=0.001` (+ `fed_truth_steps=2000` for B); paired control `d040_5b_s{s}_b20k`. Seeds 2 then 1, A then B; about 1.5-2 h each.
- *Measures and rule* (each arm against `d040_5b_s{s}_b20k`, `decide_d086.py`): D-072's, unchanged (D-056's non-inferiority on D-040's margins and a gain on at least one of: synthetic F1-F4 MAE lower by >= 3 %, women's closed-phase F1 |error| lower by >= 2 Hz, probe 11's F1 scale error halved on Hillenbrand and PTDB-TUG; D-051's length guard (2) and f0 guard (3)). Seed means, both seeds in the same direction; otherwise "not robust to the seed".
- *Reported, deciding nothing*: B against A (the ramp's own effect); the fed path's weight norms (did it learn); 5b's seed spread beside the length guard.
- *Reading*: A a candidate -> the chain's input scale was the limit, and standardised fed means go into the next run's base. B a candidate and A not -> the ramp is what lets the chain learn to use the others. Neither -> the 10-16 % frozen ceiling isn't reachable through the chain in a 4k-step branch; a joint loss or a from-scratch arm would be the next question, if any. A null here is weaker than D-072's, since D-084 says the information is there.
- Scripts: `scripts/analysis/chains/d086.sh`, `scripts/analysis/decide_d086.py`; reports `reports/analysis/d086/`.

*Result* (2026-11-07, `reports/analysis/d086/decide_d086.txt`): **neither arm is a candidate**. Both are non-inferior and pass the length and f0 guards, but neither gains on either seed: synthetic F1-F4 MAE moves by at most 0.2 %, Hillenbrand F1-F3 by at most 0.1 Hz and women's closed-phase |error| by +0.0 to +0.2 Hz, and the F1 scale error is not halved. Ramp against std: within 0.1 Hz and 0.1 %. The fed path did learn weights (norms F2-F4 0.23-0.36 for std, 0.66-1.13 with the ramp), but they don't move the outputs. Read as registered: the 10-16 % frozen-oracle gain (D-084) isn't reachable through the chain's predicted means in a 4k-step branch, with or without forcing. Predicted other formants carry little beyond what h already gives each head, as D-083 found for iVTL -> aVTL. Standardised fed means don't go into the next base; D-085's heads bundle is unaffected.

## D-087 (registered 2026-11-08; seen beforehand: D-084, the channel model's filters, the dev frame count, no D-087 result): is F4's visibility part of women's real-speech F1 error? A low-pass check on existing models

*Status*: registered (Hannah, 2026-11-08). D-084's oracle: the true F4 cuts F1's MAE by 7-8 % at the frozen ceiling. At 16 kHz F4 is always in band on clean audio (about 3.4 kHz for an 18 cm tract, about 4.7 kHz at 13 cm), so a Nyquist cut can't explain errors on full-band evaluation audio. But the channel model removes F4 on part of the training mix (telephone 300-3400 Hz; a 4-7.5 kHz low-pass on 30 % of clips), and women's F4 sits near 4-4.5 kHz. Question: **on real speech, does the model's F1 depend on F4 being visible?** If not, F4's visibility (and so the training mix's low-passed share) isn't part of the women's F1 overread.
- *Models*: `d040_5b_s1_b20k`, `d040_5b_s2_b20k` (the base D-086 was compared with). No training.
- *Data*: PTDB-TUG closed-phase frames (`cache/m3w/ptdb_egg_sub.npz`), dev speakers only (6 women, 6 men; 479 files, 9401 frames). Each file is low-passed at 16 kHz before the model with the channel model's own filter (8th-order Butterworth, causal): none, 7.5 kHz (control: the filter without removing any formant), 4.5 kHz (removes much of women's F4, keeps men's), 3.5 kHz (removes F4 for most women and part of men's). Same audio path and 400-frame windows as `add_model.py`, fp32.
- *Measures*: per sex and cutoff, the change from none in median |F1 - closed-phase F1| (Hz) with a 95 % speaker-bootstrap interval, and in median signed bias; F2 and F3 the same, reported. Reproduction check: "none" against the cached column for each model.
- *Rule* (`d087_lowpass.py`): **F4 visibility matters for women's F1** if women's F1 |error| rises by >= 2 Hz (D-086's closed-phase bar), interval above 0, at 4.5 or 3.5 kHz on both seeds. **Inconclusive** if the 7.5 kHz control moves F1 |error| by >= 2 Hz for either sex on either seed (the filter itself, not F4). Otherwise **F4 visibility does not drive women's real-speech F1**.
- *Reading*: matters -> the low-passed share of the training mix becomes a suspect for the overread (a mix arm with fewer F4-removing cutoffs, registered separately), and a wider input band (considering.md) gains a reason. Doesn't -> F4's visibility is not where the error lives; a wider band would need its own reason. Only 6 speakers per sex: a null rules out a large effect, not a small one.
- Cost: about 15-30 min (GPU). Scripts: `scripts/analysis/d087_lowpass.py`; reports `reports/analysis/d087/`.
